- **penalized-likelihood-density-ratio-fitting**: Use when many metabolite hypotheses are tested simultaneously and raw p-value FDR is inflated: you have observed t statistics (z1), log standard errors (z2), and np permutations of null statistics, and need a calibrated local fdr2d threshold for biomarker discovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/penalized-likelihood-density-ratio-fitting))
- **penalized-likelihood-estimation**: Use when you have a robust correlation/covariance matrix from single-cell gene expression data and need to distinguish direct gene-gene associations from indirect ones via a sparse concentration matrix estimate. - **penalized-logistic-regression-variable-selection**: Use when predicting a binary outcome (e.g., muscle-invasive vs non-muscle-invasive bladder cancer subtype) from thousands of genetic variables, and penalized regression with optional FDR screening is needed for variable selection and performance estimation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/penalized-logistic-regression-variable-selection))
- **penalized-probabilistic-estimation**: Use when you have a cell-by-gene scRNA-seq count matrix and want an interpretable topic-model decomposition into latent biological functions with robust (penalized) estimation.
